#AOCI-CODE-VOLUME: 1

===/<repository>/===
.gitattributes[FV5T]: F:Forces text files to check out with LF so every platform matches the raw bytes an AOCI baseline hashes | R:- | A:- | S:A CRLF checkout would mark the whole tree stale; binaries are auto-detected and left untouched
.gitignore[FV4T]: F:Excludes build output, local tooling, secrets, chart archives, runtime databases, AOCI backups, and generated Tauri directories | R:- | A:- | S:src-tauri/icons is ignored except the four build icons; ignores AGENTS.md.backup.* from AOCI init
.prettierignore[FV3T]: F:Keeps build output, vendored code, the Tauri and Go trees, bundled assets, and lockfiles out of Prettier | R:- | A:- | S:-
.prettierrc.json[FV3T]: F:Prettier options: 100-column width, two-space indent, semicolons, double quotes, trailing commas, and never rewrap prose | R:- | A:- | S:-
AGENTS.md[SG7S]: F:The repository rules for coding agents and maintainers: document map, layer ownership, development loop, verification gates, and how to call AOCI by purpose | R:- | A:- | S:.scratch/dev/ports.json is worktree-local: one loop per worktree, never copied or committed. The aoci:begin..aoci:end block is machine-owned on maintain; notes belong outside it.
CHANGELOG.md[SV5S]: F:Bilingual release notes for the current version, published as the GitHub release body and shown in the in-app changelog | R:- | A:- | S:Supports @name credits, theme-resolved colour tags, inline backticks, and fenced code blocks rendered literally
CONTRIBUTING-zh.md[SG4S]: F:Simplified Chinese contribution guide covering setup, development loop, workflow, bilingual changesets, contributor credits, and PR checks | R:- | A:- | S:Non-release PRs require a bilingual .changeset/*.md; bots and maintainers are not auto-credited
CONTRIBUTING.md[SG5S]: F:Contribution guide covering required reading, setup, workflow, bilingual changesets, contributor credits, review expectations, and source gates | R:- | A:- | S:Add EN/ZH strings together under src/i18n; non-release PRs require a bilingual changeset file
LICENSE[ZG4S]: F:The PolyForm Noncommercial License 1.0.0 text the project ships under | R:- | A:- | S:Commercial use needs a separate license, and NOTICE keeps the upstream attribution
NOTICE[ZG3T]: F:Attribution notice: author cmdysj, the Re:PhiEdit origin, the PolyForm Noncommercial terms, and the Lucide icon license | R:- | A:- | S:-
README-zh.md[SG6T]: F:The Simplified Chinese project readme: what RPE Redux is, quick start, build, web edition, and development entry points | R:- | A:- | S:-
README.md[SG6T]: F:The English project readme: what RPE Redux is, quick start, build, web edition, and development entry points | R:- | A:- | S:The desktop app is the full editor; updates, Extensions, Code Cell, collaboration hosting, and native file access stay desktop-only
eslint.config.js[XQ6M]: F:Flat ESLint config: Babel parsing for TSX with typed rules left to the compiler, React hooks, globals, and host-reach selectors | R:- | A:- | S:TS7 drops classic API so Babel parses TSX; document.* bans exempt only main.tsx and bootSplash.ts
index.html[RG4T]: F:The Vite document shell: a dark and light color scheme, the empty title, the icon link, and the root node the app mounts into | R:- | A:- | S:The title is left empty because the app sets the window title at runtime
package.json[FV6T]: F:The npm manifest: rpe-redux version, dev and build scripts, runtime dependencies, and pinned toolchain | R:- | A:- | S:Scripts split browser, desktop, web, server, font build, and source gate including check:deprecations
tsconfig.json[FV3T]: F:Strict TypeScript project settings: ES2023 with DOM libraries, bundler resolution, react-jsx, no emit, extension imports, and the included source roots | R:- | A:- | S:Strict everywhere
vite.config.ts[FV4L]: F:Vite build config: dev-runtime, theme and boot-splash injection, route base, watch exclusions, static-asset copy, and manual chunking | R:- | A:default | S:Version, base, and copied assets must match desktop host or builds fail asset requests

===/<repository>/.github/===
dependabot.yml[FV4T]: F:Weekly grouped dependency update automation for npm, cargo, gomod, and GitHub Actions, one pull request per ecosystem | R:- | A:- | S:A major update keeps its own pull request because it needs a migration review, and update pull requests pass the same source checks

===/<repository>/.github/workflows/===
audit.yml[OQ4T]: F:Weekly advisory scan of the production npm and Go dependencies, with a manual dispatch trigger | R:- | A:- | S:Runs npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high and a Go reachable-vulnerability scan on a Monday 03:00 schedule
check.yml[OQ6S]: F:The source gate on every push and pull request: it detects Rust changes, then runs the Node and Go checks | R:- | A:- | S:Interaction smoke journeys stay local because they need a built desktop executable; release packaging lives in the release workflow
codeql.yml[OQ4T]: F:CodeQL static analysis for the TypeScript application on main pushes, pull requests, and a weekly schedule | R:- | A:- | S:The Go service under src/backend is not analyzed, because CodeQL autobuild cannot see a module below the repository root
contributor-credit.yml[OV4T]: F:GitHub Actions workflow commenting computed contributor release-note credits on non-bot, non-release pull requests | R:- | A:- | S:Uses pull_request_target on base branch only; never checks out or executes PR code
pages.yml[OV4T]: F:Builds the static web edition and deploys it to GitHub Pages from the main branch | R:- | A:- | S:The browser library stores charts in OPFS, so the deployment needs no server, database, or secrets
pull-request-title.yml[OQ3T]: F:Validates a pull request title against the conventional commit form, because squash merge makes it the commit subject | R:- | A:- | S:-
release-prepare.yml[OV5S]: F:One-click release preparation: bumps shared version, rebuilds bilingual changelog with PR contributor credits, consumes changesets, and opens release PR | R:- | A:- | S:Never tags, publishes, or builds; uses RELEASE_PAT or GITHUB_TOKEN for gh CLI; GITHUB_TOKEN PRs skip source checks
release.yml[OV7L]: F:Builds and uploads the Windows and Linux desktop installers for a tag, a published release, or a manual dispatch | R:- | A:- | S:A version with a hyphen publishes as a pre-release, and the desktop app filters that flag unless the beta channel is enabled

===/<repository>/docs-sdk/en/===
api-reference.md[SE4T]: F:SDK page on API conventions: async signatures, result envelopes, direct transaction.apply commits, error codes, and stale reads | R:- | A:- | S:Every request is checked at the capability bridge and returns cloned data
capabilities.md[SE4T]: F:SDK page on capabilities: manifest permissions, media/UI capability splits, user consent, and smallest covering sets | R:- | A:- | S:Bridge re-checks every call; assets.read reads package media while assets.write targets library assets
context.md[SE4T]: F:SDK page on the extension context: the asynchronous api facade and the package surfaces beside it | R:- | A:- | S:The host calls activate once and may deactivate a package while a request is pending
design-system.md[SE3T]: F:SDK page on the design system: theme tokens, typography, colors, spacing, radii, focus rings, and motion | R:- | A:- | S:Read tokens from the host, never copy a palette
examples.md[SE4S]: F:Three complete SDK examples: toolbar-opened panel, settings row with JSON storage, and readAll/transaction.apply chart edit | R:- | A:- | S:Every registration is disposed automatically when the package is deactivated
faq.md[SE4T]: F:SDK FAQ: how to edit notes, why document and window are absent, why calls are awaited, and what a failed result means | R:- | A:- | S:An Extension runs in an opaque-origin iframe with no host DOM, Tauri IPC, network, or window.redux
getting-started.md[SE4T]: F:SDK quick start: the smallest installable Extension, from manifest and entry module to enabling it | R:- | A:- | S:The package directory name must match the manifest id, and the package lives under the user Extension root
hooks.md[SE4S]: F:SDK page on extension hooks, host events including chart:changed, and host-only lifecycle payloads | R:- | A:- | S:Public hooks coordinate packages optionally; chart writes go through transaction.apply and trigger chart:changed
panels-and-menus.md[SE4S]: F:SDK page on registering panels, sidebar entries, toolbar actions, context-menu rows, and overlays | R:- | A:- | S:The host lays out contributions; registrations live only for the active package lifetime
persistence.md[SE4T]: F:SDK page on storage surfaces: package settings, structured JSON storage, and user-visible Settings rows | R:- | A:- | S:Use the narrowest surface, and reach for the values handle when a value must be observable or bound to a control
place-note.md[SE5M]: F:SDK task guide to chart operations: read snapshots via chart.readAll, build a transaction request, and apply it | R:- | A:- | S:chart.placeNote and direct snapshot mutation are unsupported; all writes go through the transaction handle
security.md[SE4T]: F:SDK security model: the opaque-origin sandbox, capability consent groups, and the declarative UI limits | R:- | A:- | S:UI is data, not markup: the host accepts a closed tag set and scopes package CSS
types.md[SE4T]: F:SDK type glossary: handles, ApiResult, selection refs, and ChartTransactionRequest including line deletion | R:- | A:- | S:Line deletion runs before insert/update (keeping >=1 line); results wrap ok/error around transaction status
ui-reference.md[SE4M]: F:SDK reference for declarative UI nodes, persistent/modal/anchored surfaces, file/image pickers, and event contracts | R:- | A:- | S:Host owns rendering, focus, and dispatch; nodes cross the bridge as plain data

===/<repository>/src/public/assets/redux/shaders/===
bokeh.glsl[CC4T]: F:Bokeh preview effect: gaussian blur whose radius grows with distance from the focus point, driven by blurRadius, center, radius and intensity | R:- | A:- | S:Applies only inside the 0.0,0.033-0.703,0.867 sprite rect; pixels outside are sampled straight through, so that mask tracks the effect atlas
burn.glsl[CC3T]: F:Burn preview effect: wipes the texture away behind a burnColor edge as progress advances, with u_time driving the edge flicker | R:- | A:- | S:No sprite-rect test; uses the whole sampler
camera.glsl[CC3T]: F:Camera preview effect: scales, offsets and rotates the sampled texture by zoom, offset and rotation, using resolution to keep the shift proportional | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
chromatic.glsl[CC3T]: F:Chromatic preview effect: splits the texture into sampleCount colour samples pushed apart by power to fake lens dispersion | R:- | A:- | S:CC_Texture0 supplies pixels, not u_texture
chromatic_2.glsl[CC3T]: F:Second chromatic variant: same dispersion body as chromatic, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
circle_blur.glsl[CC3T]: F:Circle blur preview effect: averages samples around a radius of size pixels, using resolution to convert that radius into texture space | R:- | A:- | S:CC_Texture0 supplies pixels, not u_texture
circle_blur_2.glsl[CC3T]: F:Second circle blur variant: same averaging body as circle_blur, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
color_shift.glsl[CC4T]: F:Colour shift preview effect: converts the sample to HSV and moves hueShift, saturationShift and valueShift before converting back | R:- | A:- | S:No sprite-rect test, so it consumes the whole sampler; the three shifts are applied in HSV, never per channel
distortion.glsl[CC3T]: F:Distortion preview effect: pushes the sampling coordinate outward by power as progress sweeps the effect across the texture | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
emboss.glsl[CC3T]: F:Emboss preview effect: differences neighbouring taps scaled by strength, using resolution as the tap step | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
fisheye.glsl[CC3T]: F:Fisheye preview effect: bends the sampling coordinate away from the centre by power, aspect-corrected through resolution | R:- | A:- | S:CC_Texture0 supplies pixels, not u_texture
fisheye_2.glsl[CC3T]: F:Second fisheye variant: same bend body as fisheye, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
flare.glsl[CC3T]: F:Lens flare preview effect: sums samples along the line from the screen to lightpos and adds them scaled by power | R:- | A:- | S:No sprite-rect test; uses the whole sampler
flip.glsl[CC4T]: F:Flip preview effect: mirrors the texcoord on one axis chosen by direction, animating the mirror line with progress | R:- | A:- | S:direction picks the axis once, so a caller cannot mix a horizontal and a vertical flip in one effect
flowing.glsl[CC3T]: F:Flowing light preview effect: scrolls a coloured band of width along direction at speed, tinting by color and u_time | R:- | A:- | S:No sprite-rect test; uses the whole sampler
fog.glsl[CC2T]: F:Fog preview effect: blends fogColor in from fogStart to fogEnd on the vertical axis, drifting with u_time | R:- | A:- | S:Fog runs on texture Y, not screen space
glitch.glsl[CC4T]: F:Glitch preview effect: offsets blockCount horizontal slices at rate and speed, adding channel separation scaled by colorRate and power | R:- | A:- | S:Slice count and rate are separate uniforms, so the block pattern and its tempo are tuned independently
glitch_2.glsl[CC4T]: F:Second glitch variant: same slice body as glitch, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Differ from its sibling only in this doc block, so a fix belongs in both
glow_effect.glsl[CC3T]: F:Glow preview effect: keeps samples above threshold and spreads them by intensity over a resolution-sized kernel | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
grayscale.glsl[CC2T]: F:Grayscale preview effect: mixes the luminance-weighted sample toward grey by factor, using the 0.299 0.587 0.114 weights | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
grayscale_2.glsl[CC2T]: F:Second grayscale variant: same luminance body as grayscale, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
heat_distortion.glsl[CC4T]: F:Heat distortion preview effect: wobbles the sampling coordinate by strength at frequency, advanced by u_time | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough, so the wobble never touches the atlas margin
hologram.glsl[CC3T]: F:Hologram preview effect: stripes the sample and tints it with hologramColor, sweeping the stripes with u_time | R:- | A:- | S:Sampler is named texture, not u_texture
image_noise.glsl[CC3T]: F:Image noise preview effect: adds grain of power at density, reseeded each frame by u_time | R:- | A:- | S:No sprite-rect test; uses the whole sampler
kaleidoscope.glsl[CC3T]: F:Kaleidoscope preview effect: folds the polar angle around center into segments, rotated by angle | R:- | A:- | S:CC_Texture0 supplies pixels, not u_texture
lightning.glsl[CC4T]: F:Lightning preview effect: draws numBolts jagged bolts of width pixels with amplitude and frequency, flashing every flashDuration | R:- | A:- | S:Bolt geometry is regenerated from u_time alone, so the same time value always yields the same frame
liquid.glsl[CC2T]: F:Liquid preview effect: ripples the sampling coordinate by amplitude at frequency, advanced by u_time | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
neon.glsl[CC4T]: F:Neon preview effect: keeps samples above threshold and paints their edges with color, using resolution for the edge tap | R:- | A:- | S:Threshold and edge colour are separate uniforms, so the outline can be tuned without recompiling
night_vision.glsl[CC3T]: F:Night vision preview effect: tints the sample green and adds grain scaled by power, moving with u_time | R:- | A:- | S:No sprite-rect test; uses the whole sampler
noise.glsl[CC2T]: F:Noise preview effect: adds seed-driven grain of power to the sample | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
noise_2.glsl[CC2T]: F:Second noise variant: same grain body as noise, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
oil_painting.glsl[CC4T]: F:Oil painting preview effect: buckets sampleCount taps within radius pixels toward the most frequent colour, stepping through resolution | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough, so the bucket pass never reads the atlas margin
old_tv.glsl[CC4S]: F:Old TV preview effect: bends the image, adds scanlines and rolling noise, all timed from the single u_time uniform | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked, with the whole look driven by u_time, so the artefact mix is tuned here rather than by the chart
pixel.glsl[CC3T]: F:Pixelate preview effect: snaps the sampling coordinate to a grid of size pixels, derived from resolution | R:- | A:- | S:CC_Texture0 supplies pixels, not u_texture
pixel_2.glsl[CC3T]: F:Second pixelate variant: same grid-snap body as pixel, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
radial_blur.glsl[CC3T]: F:Radial blur preview effect: averages sampleCount taps along rays from the point centerX centerY, spreading them by power | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
radial_blur_2.glsl[CC3T]: F:Second radial blur variant: same ray-averaging body as radial_blur, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
rain.glsl[CC4T]: F:Rain preview effect: rainColor streaks of width pixels falling at speed with the given density, spanning height as u_time advances | R:- | A:- | S:Has no sprite-rect test, so it consumes the whole sampler rather than one effect region
rainbow.glsl[CC3T]: F:Rainbow preview effect: maps the sample onto a hue ramp scaled by power | R:- | A:- | S:CC_Texture0 supplies pixels, not u_texture
rays.glsl[CC4T]: F:God-rays preview effect: marches from each sample toward lightpos, accumulating exposure, decay, density and weight per step | R:- | A:- | S:Light position is a normalised uniform, so the ray origin is tuned per effect rather than fixed in the shader
scanline.glsl[CC2T]: F:Scanline preview effect: darkens alternating rows by power, scrolled by u_time | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
shockwave.glsl[CC4T]: F:Shockwave preview effect: bends sampling along a ring of width pixels around centerX centerY as progress expands it, by distortion | R:- | A:- | S:Ring geometry comes from progress and expand, so the shockwave stays a function of time rather than of paint order
shockwave_2.glsl[CC4T]: F:Second shockwave variant: same ring-bend body as shockwave, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as shockwave; only this header differs, so a fix belongs in both files
snow.glsl[CC4T]: F:Snow preview effect: snowCount flakes of size pixels drifting at speedx and speedy, tinted by color and phased by u_time | R:- | A:- | S:Has no sprite-rect test, so it consumes the whole sampler rather than one effect region
sobel.glsl[CC4T]: F:Sobel preview effect: edge-detects the sample with a 3x3 kernel stepped by resolution and scaled by strength | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough, so edges never appear at the atlas margin
two_tone.glsl[CC2T]: F:Two tone preview effect: picks color1 or color2 at threshold, flattening the sample into two bands | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
underwater.glsl[CC3T]: F:Underwater preview effect: displaces the sample with a ripple driven by u_time and resolution | R:- | A:- | S:No sampler uniform; reads CC_Texture0
vignette.glsl[CC3T]: F:Vignette preview effect: darkens by color from radius outward, with extend widening the falloff | R:- | A:- | S:CC_Texture0 supplies pixels, not u_texture
vignette_2.glsl[CC3T]: F:Second vignette variant: same falloff body as vignette, differing only in this header comment | R:- | A:- | S:Same body as its sibling; header only
wave.glsl[CC3T]: F:Wave preview effect: displaces the texcoord along direction by amplitude at frequency, advanced at speed by u_time | R:- | A:- | S:Sprite-rect masked; outside is passthrough
